Use a square canvas, the entire creature visible with a clear margin around ears, wings and tail, one readable three-quarter view, consistent moderate detail, clean contours, softly painted forms and gentle diffuse light on a plain warm ivory background. The creature occupies about three quarters of the canvas; adapt the camera distance to fit its whole silhouette. No text, labels, border, logo, people or other creatures. Keep the expression approachable; no injury, horror or violent action. Transform Gloom into a limbless marsh dragon with a blue body coiled in an S, a head with a droopy friendly face, a flower crown of four spotted brown petals on the head, long orange fronds as a mane and a tapering tail ending in a small spotted bud.
=== END ===

Use a square canvas, the entire creature visible with a clear margin around ears, wings and tail, one readable three-quarter view, consistent moderate detail, clean contours, softly painted forms and gentle diffuse light on a plain warm ivory background. The creature occupies about three quarters of the canvas; adapt the camera distance to fit its whole silhouette. No text, labels, border, logo, people or other creatures. Keep the expression approachable; no injury, horror or violent action. Reinterpret Tentacruel as an original dragon. Identity anchors to preserve or explicitly translate: broad blue cap; two large red domes plus central red disk; narrow eyes and two pointed blue mouth appendages; many long grey trailing tentacles. Stand Tentacruel upright on two thick tentacle legs ending in webbed pads, like a heron wading through a marsh. The domed cap rests on the shoulders as a hood above a short neck and a head with the two pointed blue appendages. A cloak of thin grey tentacles sweeps behind along the ground as a train. A short blue tail pokes out beneath the hood. No wings.
=== END ===

Use a square canvas, the entire creature visible with a clear margin around ears, wings and tail, one readable three-quarter view, consistent moderate detail, clean contours, softly painted forms and gentle diffuse light on a plain warm ivory background. The creature occupies about three quarters of the canvas; adapt the camera distance to fit its whole silhouette. No text, labels, border, logo, people or unrelated creatures; preserve any species-specific cluster or pouch young described below. Keep the expression approachable; no injury, horror or violent action. Transform Rhyhorn into a friendly armoured earth-drake: a low heavy four-legged grey body with overlapping scale plates, a long scaly tail, a short cone horn on the snout, narrowed red-brown eyes in a stern frown and small white fangs. Keep the large V-notched shield plate rising behind the head and stepped angular back armour with two dark oval vents. Four stout legs with white claws. No wings.
=== END ===
